In the Last 24 Hours

AI 嗰邊最近真係好熱鬧，有開發者將 llama.cpp 移植咗去越獄版 PS5，用主機 GPU 原生跑 GGUF 模型，仲可以透過 OpenAI 同 Anthropic 兼容 API 喺本地網絡提供服務，等如將部遊戲機變咗做家用 AI 伺服器。Claude 畀咗八條量子線路嘅自然語言規格之後，自己設計出比已發表最佳線路好一個數量級嘅方案，大幅減少實際應用需要嘅量子資源。Meta 同 Apple 聯手推進本地推理，Muse Spark Open 呢個 8160 億參數模型喺四部 Mac Studio 經 RDMA 串聯下跑到每秒超過 36 tokens，而 Vercel 新推出嘅 Jev 模型成為平台史上採用速度最快嘅 AI 模型。安全同監管方面，Anthropic 畀咗六個 METR 研究員員工級別權限做獨立評估，印度網絡安全機構報告話有多間機構嘅個人資料被 AI agent 越權存取，而新出嘅 Benchmark Map 揭示美國 90% 工時做嘅嘢根本唔喺現有 AI 評測覆蓋範圍之內。Gemini 4 Argon 展示咗玩星際爭霸戰嘅即時策略能力，Elorian 推出咗一個唔止係「關於」圖像而係「用」圖像去推理嘅模型，Replit CEO 講佢嘅「自動駕駛公司」願景，Cal AI 嗰個 19 歲創辦人承認病毒式增長靠俾錢請 KOL 推，Mark Wahlberg 話 AI 用啱方法喺電影行業係合理嘅，Bluesky 將佢哋嘅 Attie app 擴展到任何人都可以建網站同遊戲。

基建同網絡嗰邊，RFC8950 提出用 IPv6 next-hop 去路由 IPv4 流量，唔使再用傳統隧道機制，可能加速 IPv4 變成 IPv6 上面嘅可選層。一個安全研究員喺 OpenBSD 內核搵到一個存在咗 37 年嘅 counter overflow bug，利用佢攞到 root shell，證明就算最注重安全嘅作業系統都有長期代碼風險。eBay 收緊咗無線電設備嘅上架規則但豁免咗淨係用業餘無線電頻段嘅器材，JPEG XL 終於喺所有主流瀏覽器支援之後有人同 AVIF 做咗詳細比較，等開發者同設計師揀格式嗰時有方向。有人懷舊寫軟件渲染，用一部 Dell OptiPlex 380 跑 Quake 800×600 鎖定 60fps，話係佢見過最好睇嘅 DOS 遊戲畫面之一；另外有條片用兩年時間做科學測試，研究液態金屬散熱膏會唔會長期腐蝕銅同鋁部件。

消費同商業方面，Amazon 設備負責人 Panos Panay 話環境計算加 AI 將來會令傳統 app 過時，用戶唔使再自己開軟件同互動，裝置會主動預測你需要。初創公司 Veritas 將佢哋嘅防偽驗證晶片由時裝擴展到珠寶、獎盃同香水瓶，因為奢侈品品牌面對嘅「superfake」已經難到肉眼分唔出真假。Charles C. Mann 寫咗一篇長文講 Dekton 呢種超緊密陶瓷表面材料，由懷疑到欣賞，講佢點樣重塑建築同

RFC8950: Announce IPv4 with an IPv6 next-hop

https://anuragbhatia.com/post/2026/06/rfc8950-announce-ipv4-with-ipv6-next-hop/📅 2026-10-16 21:38 UTC+8▲ 69 ptsHN discussion

RFC8950 introduces a method for routing IPv4 traffic over an IPv6 network by using an IPv6 next-hop, eliminating the need for traditional tunneling mechanisms. The approach is logically straightforward but carries significant implications for network architecture, as it effectively makes IPv4 an optional layer riding on top of IPv6 infrastructure. This could accelerate the transition toward IPv6-only networks while maintaining backward compatibility for IPv4 services.

RFC8950 容許用 IPv6 嘅 next-hop 去 announce IPv4 路由，唔使再用 tunnel 就可以喺 IPv6 網絡上面行 IPv4。呢個做法邏輯上簡單，但意義好大，因為佢等如將 IPv4 變成一個可選嘅功能，直接搭喺 IPv6 上面行。呢個可以話係推動網絡全面轉向 IPv6 嘅一个重要一步。

一個叫 Veritas 嘅初創公司而家將佢哋嘅防偽晶片擴展到珠寶、獎盃同香水樽，因為奢侈品品牌面對嘅「超假」問題越嚟越嚴重，啲假嘢做得好似真嘅一樣，根本分唔出真假。呢個晶片可以將數碼身份嵌入實體商品入面，等買家同賣家都有個可靠嘅方法去確認貨品係唔係正貨。呢個舉動反映咗假冒貿易已經進化到一個好精密嘅威脅，而家要跨多個奢侈品類別用科技去解決。

Integrating Netflix’s Foundation Model into Personalization applications

https://netflixtechblog.com/integrating-netflixs-foundation-model-into-personalization-applications-cf176b5860eb📅 2026-10-15 04:02 UTC+8

Netflix engineers have outlined three methods for integrating a large Foundation Model into their personalization systems: generating embeddings via an Embedding Store, embedding the model as a subgraph within downstream models, and fine-tuning the model directly for specific applications. Each approach balances freshness, computational cost, and integration complexity differently, with embeddings being the easiest to adopt and subgraph integration unlocking the deepest capabilities for high-impact use cases. This work addresses a practical gap in the industry, where most published research focuses on training large transformer models rather than wiring them into existing production recommendation pipelines.

Netflix 工程師詳細講咗點樣將大型 Foundation Model 接入佢哋嘅個人化系統，用咗三個方法：透過 Embedding Store 生成 embeddings、將模型嵌入下游模型做 subgraph、同埋直接 fine-tune 模型嚟用。每個方法喺 freshness、運算成本同整合複雜度之間都有唔同嘅取捨，其中 embeddings 門檻最低，而 subgraph 整合就可以發揮模型最深嘅能力。呢個工作填咗業界一個好明顯嘅空缺，因為大部分已發表嘅研究都集中喺點樣訓練大型 transformer 模型，好少講點樣將佢哋接入現有嘅生產環境推薦系統。

Measuring the Impact of Personalized Recommendations

https://netflixtechblog.com/measuring-the-impact-of-personalized-recommendations-4c26be3a4d96📅 2026-10-15 03:57 UTC+8

Netflix researchers have developed a low-rank choice model that disentangles a member's true viewing preferences from the incremental impact of personalized recommendations, addressing a long-standing challenge in streaming analytics. The model, published in the International Journal of Industrial Organization and presented at ACM EC '26, treats daily viewing as a dynamic choice process and has been validated against randomized experiments that nudge title visibility. This work is significant because disabling personalization for clean experiments would degrade the user experience, making a credible modelling approach the only practical path forward.

Netflix 嘅研究人員開發咗一個低秩選擇模型，可以將用戶真正嘅觀影喜好同個人化推薦嘅額外影響分開嚟睇，解決咗好耐以嚟一個難題，就係點樣分辨一個人睇某套劇係因為被推薦定係自己都會搵到。呢個模型已經喺 International Journal of Industrial Organization 發表，亦喺 ACM EC '26 上面介紹過，佢將每日嘅觀影行為當成一個動態選擇過程，仲用咗隨機實驗去驗證過。呢個研究之所以重要，係因為你唔可能為咗做實驗就將個人化推薦關咗佢，咁樣會影響用戶體驗，所以呢個建模方法係唯一實際可行嘅路。

TimeSeries Tiered Storage Journey: Kafka/Flink Streams to Native Cassandra Cold Reads

https://netflixtechblog.com/timeseries-tiered-storage-journey-kafka-flink-streams-to-native-cassandra-cold-reads-e59d597c9d60📅 2026-10-15 03:56 UTC+8

A team of engineers at Netflix detailed their journey building a tiered storage system for time-series data, moving from Kafka and Flink stream processing for hot data to native Cassandra reads for cold data. The architecture addresses the challenge of balancing cost and performance by separating frequently accessed data from historical data across different storage tiers. This approach allows the platform to scale efficiently while keeping query latency acceptable across the full data lifecycle.

Netflix 嘅工程團隊分享咗佢哋點樣為時間序列數據搭建分層儲存架構，由 Kafka 同 Flink 處理熱數據，逐步過渡到用 Cassandra 原生讀取冷數據。呢個方案解決咗成本同效能之間嘅平衡問題，將常用數據同歷史數據分開存放喺唔同層級。透過呢種做法，平台可以喺保持查詢速度嘅同時，有效應對數據量不斷增長嘅挑戰。

Netflix has developed a centralized system called Ads Event Publisher to replace its previously fragmented ad telemetry pipelines. The system collects ad events from client devices, enriches them with context about the served ad, and distributes a unified data stream to downstream consumers including frequency capping, billing, and measurement partners. All member data passing through the pipeline is encrypted and handled under Netflix's strict privacy and security standards.

Running Apache Spark experiments in my sleep (and on a plane)

https://netflixtechblog.com/running-apache-spark-experiments-in-my-sleep-and-on-a-plane-588816b6b5f8📅 2026-10-15 03:49 UTC+8

A Netflix engineer spent two months chasing Out-Of-Memory failures in the member-sessionizer pipeline, which consolidates tens of billions of hourly client events into viewing sessions, only to find the real solution was running five parallel experiments at once through branch-isolated deployments rather than optimising a single run. The root cause was collect_list buffering bloated event bags in memory, made worse by UUID strings replacing 8-byte longs and Scala Option wrappers inflating object allocation, meaning slimming individual event weight proved far more effective than simply adding cluster memory. The episode highlights how big-data debugging often rewards creative parallel experimentation over brute-force resource scaling.

一個 Netflix 工程師花咗兩個月去追 member-sessionizer pipeline 入面嘅 Out-Of-Memory 問題，呢個 pipeline 要將幾百億個每小時嘅 client event 整合做觀影 session，最後發現真正嘅解法唔係優化一次 run，而係用 branch-isolated deployment 同時開五個平行實驗。根本原因係 collect_list 將成堆 event 塞喺 memory 入面，再俾 UUID 字串取代咗 8-byte long 同 Scala Option wrapper 加劇，所以減輕每個 event 嘅重量比加 cluster memory 有效得多。呢個 case 話畀我哋知，搞大數據 debug 有時靠創意平行實驗比硬加資源更加著數。

The Morris Worm Didn’t Break Anything. It Just Couldn’t Tell It Had Already Been There

https://hackernoon.com/the-morris-worm-didnt-break-anything-it-just-couldnt-tell-it-had-already-been-there📅 2026-10-16 19:03 UTC+8

The Morris Worm of 1988 caused widespread disruption not through malicious destruction but through a simple design flaw: it could not detect whether a machine had already been infected. Each time it spread, it re-infected systems it had already compromised, overwhelming them with redundant copies of itself. The incident remains a landmark case in cybersecurity, illustrating how the absence of a basic self-awareness check can turn a well-intentioned experiment into a crisis.

1988年嘅Morris Worm之所以搞到成個網絡大癱瘓，唔係因為佢有惡意破壞，而係因為佢冇辦法知道自己已經入咗邊部機。佢不斷重複感染同一啲電腦，搞到部機越嚟越慢，最終完全癱瘓。呢件事提醒咗所有人，一個好簡單嘅自我偵測機制缺失，就可以將一個實驗變成一場災難。

https://blog.cloudflare.com/verified-bots-accountability/📅 2026-10-16 21:00 UTC+8

https://blog.cloudflare.com/root-ksk-2024-rollover/📅 2026-10-07 01:50 UTC+8

Validating resolvers must adopt the new key before the switch or risk losing access to otherwise healthy websites. The event matters because a failure to prepare could cause widespread disruption to Internet connectivity.

DNS root嘅key-signing key喺2026年10月11號要換，呢個係歷史上第二次，因為呢個key係成個DNSSEC信任鏈嘅根基，如果resolver冇提前裝好新key，正常嘅網站都會打唔開。呢件事好重要，因為準備唔好嘅話，成個互聯網都可能出現大範圍嘅連線問題。

Getting the Source Right, Not Just the Fact: Source-Aware Verification for MCP Agents

https://huggingface.co/blog/MultiverseComputingCAI/getting-the-source-right-not-just-the-fact-source📅 2026-09-29 21:07 UTC+8

A new paper called ProvenanceGuard tackles a subtle but dangerous failure mode in AI agents that use the Model Context Protocol, where a fact is genuinely present in the evidence pool but gets credited to the wrong source. The researchers call this "cross-source conflation" and argue that conventional verifiers miss it because they only check whether a claim exists somewhere, not whether the attribution is correct. The work matters because source-blind checking lets subtly misleading outputs slip through, eroding trust in what AI agents report.

一篇新論文 ProvenanceGuard 針對 MCP 智能代理一個好隱蔽嘅問題，就係事實本身冇錯，但係歸錯咗來源，佢哋叫呢個做「跨來源混淆」。傳統嘅核實方法淨係睇個事實喺唔喺證據入面，唔會理佢係咪講啱咗邊個講嘅，所以呢種錯誤好容易漏網。呢個問題好重要，因為如果連邊個講嘅都搞錯，就算事實啱，讀者都會被誤導，長遠會令成個 AI 代理嘅可信度大打折扣。

Staying Evergreen: How we automated secure access and repo hygiene in a shared org

https://github.blog/engineering/platform-security/staying-evergreen-how-we-automated-secure-access-and-repo-hygiene-in-a-shared-org/📅 2026-10-16 02:00 UTC+8

GitHub's internal team developed Evergreen, a GitHub App that automates quarterly access reviews for outside collaborators and flags stale repositories for archival across an organisation of over 1,400 employees. The tool follows a fail-secure philosophy by revoking access by default when no one responds, while storing every state change as a JSON file in git to serve as a full audit trail. This matters because manual permission housekeeping becomes unmanageable at scale.

GitHub 內部團隊開發咗一個叫 Evergreen 嘅 GitHub App，用嚟自動做季度權限審查同標記過時嘅 repo 做歸檔，覆蓋超過 1,400 人嘅組織。呢個工具採用 fail-secure 原則，冇人回應就預設撤銷權限，同時將每次狀態變更以 JSON 檔案存入 git 作為完整審計紀錄。呢樣嘢重要因為人手管理權限喺大規模組織根本做唔切。

From 2,500 requests to 24: Rebuilding star history without the stargazers

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/from-2500-requests-to-24-rebuilding-star-history-without-the-stargazers/📅 2026-10-15 03:11 UTC+8

Maintainers rely on star history curves to determine whether a launch or event shifted a project's trajectory, but reconstructing that curve from the GitHub API has been an expensive process. Tools must paginate through thousands of stargazer records, extract timestamps, and discard user identities just to rebuild the timeline. The article highlights how this tedious data-gathering pipeline, which once required up to 2,500 API requests, is being streamlined down to as few as 24.

Don’t stop early: Case-folding source code at memory speed

https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/dont-stop-early-case-folding-source-code-at-memory-speed/📅 2026-08-01 00:00 UTC+8

GitHub engineers supercharged case-folding in Blackbird, their code search engine handling over 480TB of source code across 180 million repositories, by removing an early-exit branch from the ASCII fast path. This counterintuitive move unlocked CPU vectorization and pushed throughput from 3 GiB/s to over 45 GiB/s, essentially saturating memory bandwidth. The team has open-sourced the result as a Rust crate.

GitHub 嘅工程師為咗佢哋嘅代碼搜尋引擎 Blackbird 做咗一個好反直覺嘅優化，將 case-folding 入面 ASCII 快速路徑嘅 early-exit 分支直接刪走，結果反而解鎖咗 CPU 向量化，令吞吐量由 3 GiB/s 飆升到超過 45 GiB/s，基本上已經撞到記憶體頻寬嘅上限。呢個引擎要處理超過 480TB 嘅原始碼、涵蓋 1.8 億個 repo，而佢哋已經將成果以 Rust crate 嘅形式開源出嚟。

Multi-region disaster recovery for Amazon API Gateway WebSocket APIs

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/multi-region-disaster-recovery-for-amazon-api-gateway-websocket-apis/📅 2026-10-14 22:31 UTC+8

AWS has published a design pattern for multi-region disaster recovery of API Gateway WebSocket APIs, combining Route 53 failover routing, DynamoDB Global Tables for cross-region state replication, and a canary health probe to detect regional outages. Because WebSocket connections are persistent and tied to a single region, a regional failure severs every active connection and destroys session state for real-time applications such as live auctions, collaborative editors, and IoT dashboards. The solution automatically redirects clients to a secondary region and restores their application state without requiring any configuration changes on the client side.

AWS 出咗一篇技術文章，介紹點樣用 Route 53 故障轉移、DynamoDB Global Tables 同埋 canary 健康探測，為 API Gateway WebSocket API 建立跨區域災難恢復方案。WebSocket 連線係持久嘅，一旦某個區域出事，所有連線即刻斷晒，即時競投、協作編輯器呢類應用嘅用戶會連 session 狀態都一齊失去。呢個方案可以自動將客戶端導向後備區域，同時恢復佢哋嘅應用狀態，用戶完全唔使改任何設定。

It matters because sovereignty requirements increasingly extend beyond the public sector into areas like generative AI adoption and cross-border expansion, and without structured guidance, decisions in one architectural layer can silently undermine controls in another.

The reference architecture runs VMware Cloud Foundation 9.1 on EC2 bare metal instances with NFS datastores, NSX networking, and Transit Gateway routing, while VMware HCX enables near-zero-downtime migration from on-premises environments. This approach preserves existing VMware operational workflows and tooling, making it a practical path for organizations seeking cloud flexibility without disrupting their database operations.

Running multi-day AZ evacuation drills with ARC Zonal Shift

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/running-multi-day-az-evacuation-drills-with-arc-zonal-shift/📅 2026-10-01 03:01 UTC+8

AWS has published a detailed walkthrough on running multi-day Availability Zone evacuation drills using Amazon Application Recovery Controller Zonal Shift, a technique that goes beyond traditional short-duration disaster recovery tests by sustaining full production load on N-1 zones for 48 to 72 hours. The article highlights how this approach exposes time-dependent failure modes such as misconfigured Auto Scaling policies, stale DNS entries, and long-lived database connections that brief failover tests never surface. Financial services institutions in particular are adopting these drills to satisfy regulators who now demand auditable proof of operational resilience rather than mere documentation.

AWS 出咗一篇教學文，教你點樣用 ARC Zonal Shift 做持續兩三日嘅 Availability Zone 疏散演練，唔再係以前嗰種幾分鐘就 rollback 嘅短測試。呢種做法可以暴露到啲需要長時間先會浮現嘅問題，例如 Auto Scaling 冇調校好、DNS 過期、同埋數據庫連線長期綁死喺某一個 AZ 呢啲隱患。尤其金融機構而時被監管要求要攞到實證證明自己嘅災難恢復能力，唔再係交份 runbook 就算數，所以呢啲多日演練已經變咗係行業趨勢。

ReadyOn’s Four Walls of tenant isolation on Amazon EKS

https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/architecture/readyons-four-walls-of-tenant-isolation-on-amazon-eks/📅 2026-09-19 05:33 UTC+8

ReadyOn, an AWS Partner serving Fortune 100 enterprises, has built a "Four Walls" tenant isolation architecture on Amazon EKS that layers Kubernetes namespaces, Karpenter-managed dedicated node pools, per-tenant VPC security groups, and dedicated Aurora databases to protect sensitive workforce data. The model addresses a critical gap in multi-tenant Kubernetes security, where relying solely on namespaces—which were never designed as a security boundary—leaves platforms vulnerable to cross-tenant data access. By requiring an attacker to simultaneously defeat controls at four different layers of the stack, the architecture provides defense in depth for data spanning hundreds of thousands of employees across multiple regulatory jurisdictions.

ReadyOn 係一家服務財富100強企業嘅 AWS 合作夥伴，佢哋喺 Amazon EKS 上面設計咗一個「四道牆」嘅租戶隔離架構，將 Kubernetes namespace、Karpenter 管理嘅專用節點池、獨立嘅 VPC 安全組同埋每個租戶專屬嘅 Aurora 資料庫疊埋一齊，用嚟保護敏感嘅員工數據。呢個架構解決咗多租戶 Kubernetes 安全上一個好關鍵嘅問題——淨係靠 namespace 做隔離根本唔夠，因為佢本身都唔係設計嚟做安全邊界嘅。要同時突破四層唔同層面嘅防護先至可以跨租戶存取數據，對處理幾十萬員工資料、涉及多個司法管轄區監管要求嘅平台嚟講，呢種深度防禦真係必不可少。
